1b1(phal chen): @##//sangs rgyas phal bo che las/sdong pos brgyan pa'i le'u ste/
1b2(phal chen): bam po brgya gcig go/de nas tshong dpon gyi bu nor bzangs mtshan mo'i lha mo 
1b3(phal chen): sems can kun tu skyong ba'i gzi brjid dpal gyi byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par 
1b4(phal chen): thar pa'i 'jig rten thams cad mngon du gyur pas 'gro ba rnam par 'dul ba de la 
2a1(phal chen): @##//yongs su sgom zhing khong du chud par bya ba dang /yid ches par bya ba dang /'jug 
2a2(phal chen): par bya ba dang /shin tu rgyas par bya ba dang /yongs su khyab par bya ba dang /rab tu mched par bya ba dang /
2a3(phal chen): dbang du bya ba dang /snang bar bya ba dang /yang dag par gzhol bar bya ba byed cing /mtshan mo'i lha mo sgra 
2a4(phal chen): rgya mtsho rab tu zhi ba dang ldan pa gang na ba der phyin te/mtshan mo'i lha mo sgra rgya mtsho rab tu zhi ba dang /ldan pa'i rkang 
2a5(phal chen): po gnyis la spyi bos phyag 'tshal te/mtshan mo'i lha mo sgra n+ya mtsho rab tu zhi ba dang ldang pa la/lan brgya 
2b1(phal chen): stong du mar g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas nas/mtshan mo'i lha mo spu rgya mtshe rab tu zhi ba dang ldan pa'i mdun du 'dug ste/thal 
2b2(phal chen): mo sbyar nas 'di skad ces smras so//'phags ma lha mo bdag ni bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub du yang dag par 
2b3(phal chen): zhugs pa ste/bdag ni dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyi rten gyis byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la slob cing byang chub sems dpa'i 
2b4(phal chen): spyod pa la zhugs te/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod ba la nan tan du bgyi ba dang /byang chub sems dpa' spyod pa la rab tu gnas 
2b5(phal chen): pas/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du 'byung bar 'tshal na/'phags ma lha mo tha ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems 
2b6(phal chen): dpa'i spyod pa la bslab par bgyi ba dang /ji ltar nan tan du bgyi ba bdag la bshad de gsol/de nas mtshan mo'i lha mo sbya 
3a1(phal chen): @##//rgya mtsho rab tu zhi ba dang ldan pas/tshong dpon gyi bu nor bzangs la 'di skad ces smraso//rigs kyi 
3a2(phal chen): bu de ltar khyod dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyi rten gyis/byang chub sems dpa' snod pa yongs tshol ba legs so legs so//rigs 
3a3(phal chen): kyi bu kham ni byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa dga' ba yangs pa'i shugs 'byung bas sems kyi skad cig rnam par brgyan pa 
3a4(phal chen): thob pa'o//nor bzangs kyis smras pa/'phags ma khyod ni phrin las ci mdzad/yul gang dang ldan/sbyor ba gang dang ldan/
3a5(phal chen): ji la rnam par spyad/byang chub sems dpa'i rnams thar par dga' ba yangs pa'i shug sa 'byung bas/sems kyi skad cig rnam 
3a6(phal chen): par brgyan pa de'i yul ni ji lta bu zhig mchis/des smras pa/rigs kyi bu khom na sems dang bsam pa rgya mtsho yongs su dag pa'i 
3b1(phal chen): mnyam pa nyid du zhugs pa ste/'jag rten gyi rdul dri ma thams cad rnam par bsal bas mi phyed pas brgyan pa'i bsam pa la zhugs pa'o//
3b2(phal chen): brtsams pa las mi ldog/mi ldog pa'i sems dang ldan pa'o//rin po che'i ri bo ltar yon tan gyis brgyan pa'i sems mi sgul ba dang ldan 
3b3(phal chen): pa'o||mi gnas mi rten pa'i sems dang ldan pa'o//'gro ba thams cad yongs su skyabs pa la mngon par phyogs pa'i sems dang ldan pa'o/sangs 
3b4(phal chen): rgyas rgya mtsho thams cad mthong bas mi ngoms pa'i sems dang ldan pa 'o//byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi stobs kyis bsam pa rnam par dag pa'i 
3b5(phal chen): sems dang ldan pa'o//dran pa'i rgya mtsho ye shes kyi spang ba chen pos brgyan pa la yang dag par gnas pa'i sems dang ldan pa'i//sems can thams 
3b6(phal chen): cad mya ngan gyi dkon pa las yang dag par bzla ba la zhugs pa'o//sems can thams cad sdug bsngal dang /yid mi bde ba las rnam par bzlog
3b7(phal chen): pa' mngon par brtson pa'o//sems can thams chad kyi yid du mi 'ong ba la gzugs dang //sgra dang srid pa tshe dang reg yongs su rgyu ba rnam par bzlog pa la 
4a1(phal chen): @##//zhugs pa'o//sems can thams cad kyi sdug pa dang mi sdug pa dang bral zhing phrad pa'i sdug bsngal rab tu zhi bar bya ba la zhugs pa'o//
4a2(phal chen): sems can thams cad kyi yul gyi rkyen las byung bas mgo rmongs pa'i sdug bsngal rnam par bzlog pa la rab tu brtson pa'o//log par ltung ba mi sems can thams 
4a3(phal chen): cad kyi rten du gyur pa'o//sems can thams chad 'khor ba'i gnas kyi sdug bsngal las 'byung ba yang dag par ston pa la mngon par brtson pa'o//sems can 
4a4(phal chen): thams cad kyi rga shi dang mya ngan dang smre sngags 'don pa dang /sdug bsngal ba dang /yid mi bde ba dang 'khrug pa rnam par bzlog pa la zhugs pa'o//sems 
4a5(phal chen): can thams cad la de bzhin gshegs pa'i bde ba phran med pa yongs su bsgrub par zhugs pa ste/grong dang grong khyer dang /ljongs dang /yul dang khams dang /rgyal 
4a6(phal chen): po'i gnas na 'khod pa'i sems can thams cad bde bar rim gror bya bas/nyams dga' bar 'gyur zhing de dag la chos dang mthun par bsrung ba dang /bskyab pa dang /
4a7(phal chen): rim gro bya ba yang legs par byed/de dag rim pas thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su smin par byed do//'di lta ste khang khyim dang /gzhal med khang na gnas pa'i 
4b1(phal chen): sems can rnams kyi mngon par chags pa med pa yongs su sbyor//yid mi bde ba de dang de dag kyang rnam par sel to//mngon par zhen pa thams cad rgyun bcad pa dang /chos 
4b2(phal chen): thams cad kyi rang bzhin yongs su shes par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to//pha ma dang phu nu dang /snying mo dang gnyen pa shes dang 'grogs pa'i sems can yun ring po nas bshes 
4b3(phal chen): shing mdza' bar gyur pa dag las sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' dang phrad par bya ba'i phyir chos stonato//bu rmad dang 'grogs pa'i sems cad rnams la 'khor ba'i 
4b4(phal chen): sred pa thams cad rab tu sprang ba dang /sems can thams cad la mnyam pa nyid kyi sems su bya ba dang /snying rje chen po rab tu thob par bya ba'i phyir chos stonate//tshong 'dus kyi 
4b5(phal chen): dbus na 'khod pa'i sems can rnams la 'phags pa'i dge 'dun dang /de bzhin gshegs pa mthong bar bya ba la yang dag par gzhol bar bya ba'i phyir chos stonato/
4b6(phal chen): longs spyod kyi dregs pas myos pa'i sems can rnams la bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir chos stong to//glu dang phrar dang rol mo la mngon par dga' 
4b7(phal chen): ba'i sems can rnams la chos kyi dga' ba la mos par bya ba'i phyir chos sten te//yul gyi dga' ba la chags pa'i sems can rnams de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la
5a1(phal chen): @#//yang dag par gzhol bar bya ba'i phyir chos ston to//khro ba sa yongs su non pa'i sems can rnams la bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la rab tu dgod par bya ba'i phyir chos 
5a2(phal chen): ston to//sems can phi lo can rnams la brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su dag par bya ba'i phyir chos ston/sems rnam par myos pa'i sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs 
5a3(phal chen): pa'i bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa rab tu thob par bya ba'i phyir chos ston te//lta ba'i rnam pa thibs por subs pa'i sems can ma rig pa'i mun nag 'thom pa rnams la lta ba'i rnam pa 
5a4(phal chen): thibs po dang /ma rig pa'i mun pa rnam par bsal ba'i phyir chos ston to/shes rab 'chal ba can gyi sems can rnams la/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rab du thob par bya ba'i phyir 
5a5(phal chen): chos ston to//khams gsum pa la mngon par zhon pa'i sems can rnams la'akhor ba las 'byung bar bya ba'i phyir chos ston to//dma' ba la mos pa'i sems can rnams la/de bzhin 
5a6(phal chen): gshegs pa'i byang chub tu smon lam yongs rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir chos stonato//bdag la phan par brtson pa'i sems can rnams la sems can thams cad la phan par bya ba'i smon 
5a7(phal chen): lam yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur bar bya ba'i phyir chos stonato//bsam pa nyam chung ba'i sems can rnams la byang chub sems dpa'i stobs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rnam par dag par bya 
5b1(phal chen): ba'i phyir chos ston to//ma rig pa'i mdun pas sems glong ba'i sems can rnams la/byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes kyi ba bor du phyin pa rnam par dag par bya ba'i phyir chos ston te//
5b2(phal chen): gzugs mi sdug pa dang ldan pa'i sems can rnams la/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzugs kyi sku rnam par dag par bya ba'iphyir chos ston to//lus kyi dbyings mi mdzes pa dang ldan pa'i 
5b3(phal chen): sems can rnams la chos kyi sku bla na med pa rnam par dag par bya pa'i phyir chos ston to//kha dog mi sdug pa dang ldan pa'i sams can rnams la/de bzhIn gshegs pa'i sku mdog gser 
5b4(phal chen): gyi kha dog lta bu dang /shin tu 'jam zhing ka tsa li bda' ltar reg na bde ba'i lus rnam par dag par bya ba'-i phyir chos ston to//sdug bsngal ba'i sems can rnams la de bzhin 
5b5(phal chen): gshegs pa'i bde ba dam pa rab tu thob pa'i phyir chos ston to/bde ba'i sems can rnams la thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bde ba rab tu thob par bya ba'i phyir chos stonato//nad pa na ba'i 
5b6(phal chen): sems can rnams la/byang chub sems dpa'i lus gzugs brnyan lta bu yongs grub par bya ba'i phyir chos stong to//dga' ba sna tshogs la brtson pa'i sems can rnams la/byang
5b7(phal chen): chub sems dpa'i spyod pa'i dga' ba rab tu thob par bya pa'i phyir chos stong to//tshangs pa'i sems can rnams la byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi gter dang mdzod rab tu thob par bya ba ni
6a1(phal chen): @#//phyir chos ston to//pha zal shing gi dban gnas pa'i sems can rnams la/sangs rgyas kyi chos yongs su btsal ba mngon par brtson pa'i rgyu thob par bya ba'i phyir chos ston 
6a2(phal chen): to//lam du thugs pa'i sems can rnams la /thams cad mkhyen pa'i lam du 'jug par bya ba'i phyir chos stonato//grong na gnas pa'i sems can de rnams la/khams gsum 
6a3(phal chen): pa thams cad las 'byung bar bya ba'i phyir chos stonate//yul gyi med pa na gnas pa'i sems canarnams la/nyan thos dang rang byang chub kyi lam las yang dag par 'da' bar bya ba 
6a4(phal chen): dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa la rab tu dgod par bya ba'i phyir chos stonati//grong rdal na 'khod pa'i sems can rnams la/chos kyi rgyal po'i pho brang gnas par 'gyur ba'i 
6a5(phal chen): phyir chos ston to//phyogs mtshams na 'khod pa'i sems canarnams la/dus gsum mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes rab tu thob par bya ba'i phyir ches stonato//phyogs na gnas pa'i sems 
6a6(phal chen): can rnams la/chos thams cad kyi ye shes mngon par shes par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to//'dod chags la gcig tu dga' ba'i sems can rnams la mi sdug pa'i sgos 'khor ba'i 
6a7(phal chen): sred pa rnam par bzlog pa'i phyir chos ston to//sha ldang ba spyod pa'i sems can rnams la byams pa chen po'i tshul rgya mtsho la 'jug par bya ba'i phyir chos stonato//gti mug la spyod 
6b1(phal chen): pa'i sems can rnams la chos thams cad kyi sgo rgya mtsho rab tu 'byed pa'i ye shes mngon par shes par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to//ma mnyam pa la spyod pa'i sems can rnams la/theg 
6b2(phal chen): pa thams cad kyi smon lam gyi tshul rgya mtshos khyad par du 'gro ba'i bya ba'i phyir chos ston te//'khor ba'i yul gyi dga' ba'i bsam pa can gyi sems can rnams la/'khor ba'i yul 
6b3(phal chen): dga' ba rnam par bzlog pa'i phyir chos ston to//'khor ba'i sdug bsngal thams cad kyis gtsos pa'i sems can rnams la 'khor ba'i sdug bsngal thamscad kyis nye bar nyon mongs 
6b4(phal chen): pa med par bya ba'i phyir chos stonato//de bzhin gshegs pas 'dul ba'i sems can rnams la/mi skye ba'i gnas yang dag par bstan pa'i phyir chos stonato//phung po la gnas pa'i 
6b5(phal chen): bsam pa can gyi sems canarnams la/gzhi med pa'i chos kyi spyod yul la rnam par spyod par bya ba'i phyir chos stong te//bsam pa zhum pa'i sems can rnams lalam gyi rgyan gyi 
6b6(phal chen): khyad par yang dag par bstan par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to//mtho ba'i nga rgyal can gyi sems can rnamasal chos thams cad mnyam na nyid kyis zad pa yang dag par bstan pa'i yir chos
6b7(phal chen): stonato//g.yo sgyu'i yul gyi bsam pa dang ldan pa'i sems can rnams pa/byang chub sems dpa'i bsam pa yongs su dag par bya ba'i phyir chos stonataM//de ltar rigs kyi bu kho mo ni 
7a1(phal chen): @#//sems can thams cad chos kyi sprin pas yongs su bsdus shing ngan song gi lam gyi rgyud sdug bsngal ba thams cad nas/rnam par bzlog pa dang lha dang mi'i 
7a2(phal chen): phun sum tshogs pa dang /bde ba yongs su bstan pa dang /khams gsum pa las gyen du bskyod pa dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la rab tu dgod pa dang /thabs sna tshogs kyis 
7a3(phal chen): yongs su smin par byed byed pas dka' ba chen po'i shugs kya mtsho snang ba rab tu thob pas dga' zhing spro la yid bde bar gyur pa'o//gzhan yang rigs kyi bu kho mo ni phyogs dang /
7a4(phal chen): phyogs mtshams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho la rnam par lta zhing smon lam rgya mtsho la spyod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' lus sna tshogs rnam par dag 
7a5(phal chen): pa 'od kyi dkyil 'khor sna tshogs kyi rgyun dang ldan pa 'od gzer dang 'od kyi dkyil 'khor kha dog mtha' yas pa rab tu 'gyed pa/thams cad mkhyen pa nyidakyi tshul rgya mtsho sna chog 
7a6(phal chen): la zhugs pa'i ye shes snang ba can ting 'dzin rje mtsho sna tshogs la zhugs pa rnam par 'phrul pa sna tshogs kyi yul dang ldan pa/dbyangs kyi yan lag dang /skad rgyan che sna 
7a7(phal chen): tshogs kyi dbyangs dang ldan pa/rgyan sna tshogs kyi 'khrul par spungs pa'i lus can/de bzhin gshegs pa'i tshul sna tshogs la zhugs pa zhing rgya mtsho 'byams klas pas sna 
7b1(phal chen): tshogs su zhugs pa'i lus dang ldan pa/sangs rgyas rgya mtsho sna tshogs la zhugs pa tha dad pa yang dag pa bshes pa rgya mtsho sna tshogs la zhugasapa de bzhin gshegs pa 'jam par thar 
7b2(phal chen): pa dang /ye shes kyi 'dul sna tshogs kyis snang bar byung ba/ye shes rgya mtsho sna tshogs kyi snang ba rab tu thob pa/ting 'dzin rgya mtsho sna tshogs kyi tshul la rnam par spyod pa chos sna tshogs 
7b3(phal chen): kyis rnam par thar pa'i gzhan med khang rnam par rtse ba'i yul dang ldan pa/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi sgo sna tshogs la mngon du gnas pa chos kyi dbyings sna tshogs kyi nam mkha'i rkyen 
7b4(phal chen): dang ldan pa/rgyan sna tshogs kyi sprin gyis nam mkha' rgyas pas 'gengs pa/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho sna tshogs la rnam par lta ba/'jig rten gyi khams sna tshogs nam 'dus pa/
7b5(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi zhing sna tshogs 'byam klas par rjes dgu dong ba phyogs rgya mtsho sna tshogs nas yongs su 'dus pa/de bzhin gshegs pa tha dad pas bkye ba/de bzhin gshegs pa tha 
7b6(phal chen): dad pa'i zhabs kyi drung nas lhags pa/byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs sna tshogs kyi 'khor dang ldan pa/rgyan sna tshogasakyi sprin gyi char rab tu 'bebs pa/de bzhin gshegs ba'i tshul 
7b7(phal chen): sna tshogs la zhugs pa/de bzhin gzhig pa'i chos rgya mtsho sna tshogs la rnams par spyod pa ye shes sna tshogs rgya mtsho la zhugs pa/brgyan gyi snying po sna tshogs can gyi stan la 
8a1(phal chen): @#//'khor ba rnams kyi dga' ba sna tshogs kyi shugs rgya mtsho yongs su skyed de/de dag dga' ba sna tshogs kyi shus rgya mtsho yongs su skyes nas/de bzhin gshegs 
8a2(phal chen): pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtshor 'dod ste/de dag de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs tshad med pa rjes su sems pas kyang /dga' ba'i shugs chen po rgya mtsho yongs su 'byung bar 'gyur//
8a3(phal chen): rigs kyi bu gzhan yang /kho mo ni bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi gzugs kyi sku yongs su dag pa mtshan gyis rab tu brgyan pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa/mthong nas dga' ba chen po 
8a4(phal chen): dang|chos shing nyams bde ba rab tu 'chol ste/sems kyi skad cig re re la'ang 'od kyi dkyil 'khor chos kyi dbyings ltar yangs pa kha dog rgya mtsho mtha' dang dbung med par snang ba 
8a5(phal chen): la lta zhing sems kyi skad cig re re la dga' ba chen po'i shugs rgya mtsho rab tu 'thob//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang bcom ldan 'das rnam par/snang mdzad kyi sku'i ba spu'i sgo re re nas kyang 
8a6(phal chen): 'od gzer chen po sangs rgyas kyi zhing mtha' dang dbung med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed byung ste/'od gzer re re zhing yang /'od gzer rgya mtsho sangs rgyas kyi zhing mtha' yas pa'i rdul 
8a7(phal chen): shin tu phra ba snyed kyi 'khor dang ldan pas/chos kyi dbyings thams cad rgyas par 'gengs pa dang /sems can thams cad kyi sdug bsngal rab tu zhi bar mdzad pa mthong zhing sems kyi 
8b1(phal chen): skad cig re re la/dga' ba chen po'i shugs rgya mtsho rab tu 'jog po//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang /ba tsam ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi dbu dang /sgyu phrag pa gnyis kyi lhun las/sems 
8b2(phal chen): kyi skad cig re re la rin po che thams cad kyi 'od 'phro ba'i ri'i sprin/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba rnyed 'byung zhing chos kyi dbyangs rgya mtsho thams cad rgyas par 'gengs pa 
8b3(phal chen): mthong nas dga' ba chen po'i shugs brgya mtsho shin tu yangs pa rib tu 'thob bo//rigs kyi bu gzhan yangabacom ldan 'das rnam par snangamadzad kyi sku'i ba spu'i sgo re re nas sems kyi skad cig 
8b4(phal chen): re la kha dog de zhim pa dang /'od gzer sna tshogs can gyi sprin/sangs rgyas kyi zhi nga thams cad kyi rdul shing tu phra ba snyed 'byung zhing /sangs rgyas kyi zhing tham cad rgyas par 'gengs ba 
8b5(phal chen): mthong nas/dga' ba chen po'i shugasargya mtsho shin tu yangs par btu 'thob pa//rigs kyi bu gzhan yar bcom ldan 'das rnam parasnang mdzad kyi sku la rnam par bltas na/sems kyi 
8b6(phal chen): skad cig re re la mtshan re re nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku gzugs mtshan gyis rab tu brgyan pa'i sprin/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed byung ste/'chi brten gyi 
8b7(phal chen): kha yas rgya mtsho thams cad rgyas par 'gengs pa mdzod nas/dga' ba chen po'i shu+egs rgya mtsho shin tu yangs pa rab tu 'thob bo//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang bcom ldan 'das rnam pa 
9a1(phal chen): @#//snang mdzad kyi sku 'dab byed bzang po re re las/sems kyi skad cig re re la de bzhin gshegs pa'i sprul pa'i sku dang pa 'di bzang po brgyad cus shin tu 'bar 
9a2(phal chen): ba'i sprin/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phrad snod byung ste/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thamasacad rgyas par 'gengs shing /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi 
9a3(phal chen): 'khor lo'i dbyangs su skrag pa'i 'brug sgra 'byin pa mthong nas/dka' 'tshen po'i shugs rgya mtsho shin tu yangs pa rab tu 'thob bo//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang bcom ldan 'das rnam 
9a4(phal chen): par snang mdzad kyi sku la rnam par bltas na/sems kyi skad cig re rel ba spu'i sde thams cad nas/sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul pa'i sku dang po byang chub tu thugs bskyed pa dang bcas 
9a5(phal chen): pa rol tu phyin pa'i lam rnam par dag pa'i rkyen dang bcas/byang chub sems dpa'i sa yongs su gnon pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang /bcas pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i rdul 
9a6(phal chen): shin tu phra ba snyed 'byung ba mthong nas/dga' ba chen po'i shugs rgya mtsho shinatu yangs pa rab tu 'thob po//rigs kyi bu gzhan yangabacom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi

23b3(phal chen): du 'pho ba med pa'i lus dang /sgrib pa thams cad shin tu bcom pa'i lus dang /de bzhin nyid kyi rang bzhin gyi lus dang /'gro ba thamasacad gdul ba mthar phyin par byed pa'i lus su 
23b4(phal chen): 'dug cing mtshan mo'i lha mo brjod du med pa'i 'khor gyi bskor te/'dug pa mthong nas dga' rangs te mgu zhing nyams bde la tshim zhing yid bde ba skyes nas/mtshan mo'i lha mo grong khyer 
23b5(phal chen): thams cad bsrung ba 'byung ba'i rkang pa gnyis la spyi bos phyag 'tshal te/mtshan mo'i lha mo grong khye thams cad bsrung ba 'byungs pa'i gzi brjid dpal la/lan brgya stong du mar g.yas 
23b6(phal chen): phyogs su bskor ba byas nas/mtshan mo'i lha mo grang khyer thams cad bsrung ba 'byang ba'i gzi brjid dpal gyi mdun du 'dug te/rngal mo sbyar nas 'di skad ces smras so//lha mo bdag bla 
23b7(phal chen): na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yang dag par zhugs na/lha mo ji ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la rjes su bslabs na/sems can la sman par 
24a1(phal chen): @#//'gyur ba lags/ji ltar na sems can bla na med pa yong su bsdu bas yongs su sdud par 'gyur/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pas gnang 
24a2(phal chen): pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i las la brtson pa lags/ji ltar brtson pa rab kyis na/byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi rgyal por nye bar 'gyur ba bdag la bshad du gsol/de skad ces 
24a3(phal chen): smras pa dang /mtshan mo'i lha mo grong khyer thams cad bsrung ba 'byung ba'i gzi brjid dpal gyis/tshong dpon gyi bu nor bzangs la 'di skad ces smraso//rigs kyi bu khyod de ltar 'gro 
24a4(phal chen): ba thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba dang /rnam par gdul ba'i mthar phyin par bya ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rigs yang dag par bzung ba'i sbyor ba mthar phyin par bya 
24a5(phal chen): ba dang|phyogs 'byam klas pa thams cad rgyas par dgang ba'i ye shes rab tu sbyar ba dang /chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul rgya mtsho thams cad la 'jug pa mngon du 'gyur ba dang /shes bya 
24a6(phal chen): nam mkha'i dbyibs dang /mnyam par mtha' yas pa rgyas par dgang ba dang /de bzhin gsheg pa thams cad kyi chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu blang zhing yongs su bzung ba'i phyir/'gro ba ?
24a7(phal chen): mtsho thams cad kyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du chos kyi sprin gyi char mngon par dbab pa'i spyod pa'i tshul yongs su 'dri ba legs so legs so//rigs kyi bu kho mo ni byang chub sems 
24b1(phal chen): dpa'i rnam par thar pa sgra yid du 'ong ba dang /rnam par 'khrul ba zab mo la 'jug pa thob pa ste/de ltar ngo mo rnam par thar pa de dang ldan pas/chos smra ba chen po'i go 'phang thogs pa med 
24b2(phal chen): pa la dmigs pa'i sbyor ba dang ldan pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi mdzod yongs su btang ba'i smon lam dang ldan pa/snying rje chen po dang ldan pa byams pa chen po'i stobs rab tu thob 
24b3(phal chen): pa sems can thams cad byang chub kyi sems la dgod pa'i phyir/sems can thams cad kyi don byang la zhugs pa/byang chub kyi sems kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba dang /tshogs rab tu bsags pa

24b6(phal chen): pa'i sems 'jug pa'i rgyun mi 'chad pa/'gro ba thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba mngon par 'du bya ba yongs su bsgrub pa'i sbyor ba shes par bya ba'i phyir/sems rnam par dag pa'i sbyor ba dang ldan 
24b7(phal chen): pa|dge ba'i las kyi lam thams cad 'thun par sgrub pas/sems can thams cad gyi ded dpon du zhugs pa/mi dge ba'i las kyi lam thamasacad rab tu spangs pa/sems can thams can dge

25a2(phal chen): theg pa'i rgyan gyi thog mar 'dren pa la zhugs pa/dge ba'i chos kyi spyod pa thams cad la sems can thams cad rab tu dgod pa'i sbyor ba dang ldan pa dge ba'i bshes gnyen thams

25a5(phal chen): ba gtan zhing mi shigs pa/rdo rje mthu bo che ltar bstan zhing srabs sangs rgyas kyi stobs la dmigs pa'i sems rgya chen po'i dkyil 'khor dang ldan par dge ba'i bshes gnyen 
25a6(phal chen): la rten cing rnam par sbyor do//kho mo nyin mongs pa dang /las kyi sgrib pa thams cad kyi de rnam par sbyor ba'i sems dang ldan pa'o//thams cad mkhyen pa nyid gyi tshogs yang dag

25b5(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad du/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa yongs bstan pa'i phyir chos kyi dbyings zhes mtshams med par khong du chud pa'i byed do//de bzhin gshog 
25b6(phal chen): pa'i ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor tha mi dad pa la 'jug par bya ba'i phyir chos kyi 'byings ni ris chad pa med par khong du chud ba 'byed do//bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du sems can thams cad kyis 
25b7(phal chen): rnam par dmigs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung gi dkyil 'khor la 'jug par bya ba'i phyer chos kyi dbyings ni rgyud kyi tshul gcig tu gyur par khong du chud par byed do//'gro ba 
26a1(phal chen): @#//thams cad rnam par gdul ba'i mthar phyin pas/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sngon gyi smon lam gyi mthar thug pa la 'jug par bya ba'i phyir/chos kyi dbyings 
26a2(phal chen): ni rang bzhin gyis rgya che bar khong du chud par byed do//byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa 'bum klas pa la 'jug par bya ba'i ba'i phyir/chos kyi dbyingasani 'gro ba thams cad 
26a3(phal chen): la mnyam pa nyid du byung bar khong du chud par byedado//kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba'i rgyan la 'jug par bya ba'i phyiro/chos kyi dbyings ni rgyan cig tu gyur par

26a5(phal chen): med par khong du chud par byed de//rigs kyi bu de ltar kho mo rnam pa bcu po de dag gis dge ba'i rtsa ba dang /tshogs thams cad yongs su bsags pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi bdag 
26a6(phal chen): nyid che ba nyid la 'jug par bya ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi yul bsam gyis mi khyab pa rjes su rtag lta bar bya ba'i phyir/chos kyi dbyings thams cad la rnam par blta ba'o//khong du 
26a7(phal chen): chud pa dang rjes su gzhol bar byed do//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang kho mo da ltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i bdag nyid che ba nyid kyi rjes su 'brang ba'i yid la bya ba dang ldan pas/gzung 
26b1(phal chen): kyi dkyil 'khor gyi rgyan stong phrag bcus sems can rnams la chos ston te/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar chos rgya mtsho thams cad yang dag par 'du ba zhes bya ba'i gzungs kyi dkyil 
26b2(phal chen): 'khor dang /chos thams cad byin gyis rlob pa'i gzungs kyi dkyil 'khor dang /chos kyi sprin thams cad yongs su 'dzin pa'i gzungs kyi dkyil 'kho dang /de bzhin gshegs pa 
26b3(phal chen): thams cad kyi dgongs pa sgron ma'i gzungs kyi dkyil 'khor ba dang /sems can thams cad kyi las rgya mtsho rab tu snang bar byed pa'i snying po'i gzungs kyi dkyil 'khor rang /theg pa'i

26b6(phal chen): pa la yang dag par gzhol pa'i gzungs kyi dkyil 'a khor dang /chos thams can la mngon par 'khyil ba'i shugs drag po'i gzungs kyi dkyil 'khro dang /thams cad mkhyen 
26b7(phal chen): pa nyid kyi shugs rab tu 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i gzungs kyi dkyil 'kho dang /rigs kyi bu gzungs kyi dkyil 'khor dang bcu po de dag la sogs pa gzungs kyi dkyil 'khor gyi rgyud 
27a1(phal chen): @#//steng phrag bcus sems can can rnams la chos ston to//rigs kyi bu gzhan bdag po bsod nams thos pa'i shes rab kyis sems can rnams la chos ston te//

27a3(phal chen): ston to//srid pa'i rgyun rgya mtsho thams cad las kyang bsdums shing sem can rnams la chos snyon to//de bzhin gshegs pa gcig gi mtshan gyi 'khor lo rgya mtsho las

28a1(phal chen): @#//sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa la phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i bskal pa rgya mtshor gnas par bya bas/byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa sgra yid du 'ong

28a6(phal chen): gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi sems kyi rdul shin tu phra ba ster kyi rtsol blangs pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sngon gyi smon lam dbyangs su sgrog pa'i padamos mtshams rnam par brgyan

29b2(phal chen): de'i tshe 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po nyi ma ltar bzhin mdog dri ma med pa de nyid kyi bu mo dge slong ma chos kyi 'khor los sprul pa'i 'od ces bya ba dag slong ma brgya phrag stong gi 'khor dang

29b7(phal chen): ba'i 'od kyi smon ma des bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa sgra 'od du 'ong ba dang rnam par sprul pa zab mo la 'jug pa 'di yang shin tu phra zhing 'jam pa'i 
30a1(phal chen): @#//rdul du rab tu thob par gyur to//de thob pas de la de bzhin gshegs pa chom rgya mtsho thams cad kyi dbyangs 'od kyi rgyal po'i rnam par 'phrul ba de dag thams cad 
30a2(phal chen): kyang mngon du gyur to//rigs kyi bu de la ji snyam du sems/de'i tshe de'i dus na 'khor los sbyar ba'i rgyal po nyi ma ltar bzhin mdo ga dri ma med pa zhes bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa chos 
30a3(phal chen): rgya mtsho thams cad kyi dbyang 'od kyi rgyal po'i bstan pa la rab tu byung ste/chos kyi 'khor lo rjes su bskor ba dang /yongs su mngan las 'das nas kyang bstan pa nub tu che ba'i tshe dang 
30a4(phal chen): dus na/bstan pa yongs su 'dzin cing chos sgron ma chen po 'bar bar byed pa de gzhan zhig yin pa tsam du sems na ni/rigs kyi bu khyod kyis de lta mi blta ste/byang chub sems dpa' 
30a5(phal chen): kun tu bzang po ni/de'i tshe de'i dus na 'khar los sgrub pa'i rgyal po nyi ma ltar bzhi mdog dri ma med pa zhes bya par gyur to//rigs kyi bu del ji snyam du sems/de'i cho de'i dus na
